
### Experiments

Session rule: append every meaningful experiment here (or an appropriate file) with correctness, timing, and merge decision.

**Note on backfilled rows:** rows whose `Decision` column begins with `BACKFILL from notes/<file> —` were reconstructed post-hoc by scanning `notes/` (design docs, session logs, LaTeX reports) after this table was in place. Numbers and dates in those rows are as reported in the source note; `Time` is `--:--` because exact clock time was not recorded. Backfilled rows live in a dedicated **Backfilled from notes/** section at the bottom of this file so the source of each row remains explicit.

Logging format:
- Use local `HH:MM` time from `date` when adding new rows.
- Use `--:--` only for older rows where exact time was not recorded; do not invent precision.
- Fill both `Motivation` and `What Tried / Evidence`.
- `Motivation` should say why this was plausible before running it.
- `What Tried / Evidence` should say what changed and what the result taught us.
- Keep timings in `HH:MM` format.
